## Keep Candidates
- Schemas, docs, reports, OCR prompts, structured staging files, importer scripts, normalizer scripts, and small wiki text exports.

## Review Before Removing
- Large APK, archive, binary, duplicate import, generated cache, Unity build artifact, node_modules, venv, .git, logs, temp files, and copied full project directories.

## Next PM Recommendation
- If remote contains d09ec3b and repo size is acceptable, proceed to Phase 1C read-only data audit.
- If remote does not contain d09ec3b, finish push first.
- If repo size is excessive, create a cleanup commit that removes only obvious generated/binary/archive duplicates after explicit PM review.
